\displaystyle\|\Lambda_{0}f\|_{X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma+1}(\Omega)}+\sum_{i=1}^{d}\|\Lambda_{i}f\|_{X_{p,\theta-p}^{\gamma+1}(\Omega)}\leq N\|f\|_{X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}

(A.30)

where
N
depends only on
d
,
p
,
\gamma
,
\theta
.

Proof.

Recall (
A.2
) and (
A.3
).
Put

L_{0}=(1-\Delta)^{-1}\quad\text{and}\quad L_{i}=-D_{i}(1-\Delta)^{-1}\qquad\text{for}\,\,i=1,\,\ldots,\,d\,,

which are linear operators on
X_{p}^{\gamma}
.
It is implied by element properties of
X_{p}^{\gamma}
that for any
g\in X_{p}^{\gamma}
,

\displaystyle L_{0}g+\sum_{i=1}^{d}D_{i}L_{i}g=g\quad\text{and}\quad\sum_{i=0}^{d}\|L_{i}g\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma+1}}\lesssim_{d,p,\gamma}\|g\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\,.

(A.31)

Put

\displaystyle\Lambda_{0}f(x)\,

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\zeta_{1,(n)}(x)L_{0}\Big[\big(\zeta_{0,(n)}f\big)(e^{n}\cdot)\Big](e^{-n}x)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{n}\big(D_{i}\zeta_{1,(n)}\big)(x)L_{i}\Big[\big(\zeta_{0,(n)}f\big)(e^{n}\cdot)\Big](e^{-n}x)

\displaystyle=R_{\zeta_{1}}\big(L_{0}S_{\zeta_{0}}f\big)(x)-\sum_{i=1}^{d}\big(D_{i}\widetilde{\rho}\big)(x)\cdot R_{\eta_{0}}\big(L_{i}S_{\zeta_{0}}f\big)(x)\,,

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i}f(x)\,

\displaystyle=\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{n}\zeta_{1,(n)}(x)L_{i}\Big[\big(\zeta_{0,(n)}f\big)(e^{n}\cdot)\Big](e^{-n}x)

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\rho}(x)\cdot R_{\eta_{1}}\big(L_{i}S_{\zeta_{0}}f\big)(x)\qquad\qquad\text{for}\quad i=1,\,\ldots,\,d\,,

where
\eta_{0}(t):=\big(\zeta_{1}^{\prime}\big)(t)
,
\eta_{1}(t):=t^{-1}\zeta_{1}(t)
, and

\displaystyle\begin{gathered}L_{i}\{f_{n}\}:=\{L_{i}f_{n}\}\quad\text{for}\,\,\,\{f_{n}\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\in l_{p}^{\theta/p}(H_{p}^{\gamma})\,.\end{gathered}

Due to (
A.31
), we have

\displaystyle\Lambda_{0}f+\sum_{i=1}^{d}D_{i}\Lambda_{i}f=\,

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left(\zeta_{1,(n)}(\,\cdot\,)\times\Big[\big(L_{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{d}D_{i}L_{i}\big)\big[(\zeta_{0,(n)}f)(e^{n}\cdot)\big]\Big](e^{-n}\,\cdot\,)\right)

\displaystyle=\,

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big[\zeta_{1,(n)}\zeta_{0,(n)}f\Big]=f\,.

Therefore we only need to prove (
A.30
).
Due to (
A.6
), (
A.31
), and Proposition
A.3
.(5), we have

\displaystyle\|\Lambda_{0}f\|_{H_{p,\theta}^{\gamma+1}(\Omega)}+\sum_{i=1}^{d}\|\Lambda_{i}f\|_{H_{p,\theta-p}^{\gamma+1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{N}

\displaystyle\|R_{\zeta_{1}}\big(L_{0}S_{\zeta_{0}}f\big)\|_{H_{p,\theta}^{\gamma+1}(\Omega)}+\sum_{i=1}^{d}\Big(\|R_{\eta_{0}}\big(L_{i}S_{\zeta_{0}}f\big)\|_{H_{p,\theta}^{\gamma+1}(\Omega)}+\|R_{\eta_{1}}\big(L_{i}S_{\zeta_{0}}f\big)\|_{H_{p,\theta}^{\gamma+1}(\Omega)}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{N}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{d}\|L_{i}S_{\zeta_{0}}f\|_{l_{p}^{\theta/p}(H_{p}^{\gamma+1})}\,\,\lesssim_{N}\,\,\|S_{\zeta_{0}}f\|_{l_{p}^{\theta/p}(H_{p}^{\gamma})}\,\,\lesssim_{N}\,\,\|f\|_{H_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\,.

Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

Recall that for a regular Harnack function
\Psi
on
\Omega
,

\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega):=\big\{f\,:\,\Psi^{-1}f\in X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)\big\}\quad\text{and}\quad\|f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}:=\|\Psi^{-1}f\|_{X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\,.

Lemma A.6
.

Let
\eta\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
satisfy

\eta=1\,\,\,\,\text{on}\,\,B(0,1/2)\,\,,\quad\text{supp}(\eta)\subset B(0,1)\,\,,\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\eta\,\mathrm{d}x=1\,.

For
i\in\mathbb{N}
, let
N(i)\in\mathbb{N}
satisfy

\text{supp}\Big(\sum_{|n|\leq i}\zeta_{0,(n)}\Big)\subset\big\{x\in\Omega\,:\,\big(N(i)/2\big)^{-1}\leq\rho(x)\leq N(i)/2\big\}\,.

Let
\Lambda_{i}
,
\Lambda_{i,j}
,
\Lambda_{i,j,k}
are linear functionals on
\mathcal{D}^{\prime}(\Omega)
defined as

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i}f:=\Big(\sum_{|n|\leq i}\zeta_{0,(n)}\Big)f\,\,\,,\,\,\,\,\Lambda_{i,j}f(x)=\eta(j^{-1}x)\Lambda_{i}f(x)\,\,\,,\,\,\,\,\Lambda_{i,j,k}f=\big(\Lambda_{i,j}f\big)^{(N(i)^{-1}k^{-1})}\,,

where

\displaystyle h^{(\epsilon)}(x):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h(x-\epsilon y)\eta(y)dy:=\Big\langle h,\epsilon^{-d}\eta\big((x-\cdot)/\epsilon\big)\Big\rangle\,.

Then for any regular Harnack function
\Psi
, the following hold:

(1)

For any
f\in\mathcal{D}^{\prime}(\Omega)
,
\Lambda_{i,j,k}f\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\Omega)
.

(2)

For any
f\in\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)
,

\displaystyle\sup_{i}\|\Lambda_{i}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\leq N_{1}\|f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\,\sup_{j}\|\Lambda_{i,j}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\leq N_{2}\|f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\,\sup_{k}\|\Lambda_{i,j,k}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\leq N_{3}\|f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}

where
N_{1}
,
N_{2}
,
N_{3}
are constants independent of
f
.

(3)

For any
f\in\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)
,

\displaystyle\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}\Lambda_{i,j,k}f=\Lambda_{i,j}f\,\,\,,\,\,\,\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\Lambda_{i,j}f=\Lambda_{i}f\,\,\,,\,\,\,\lim_{i\rightarrow\infty}\Lambda_{i}f=f\quad\text{in}\quad\Psi H_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)\,.

Proof.

(1) It follows directly from properties of distributions.

(2), (3)
Note the following elementary properties of
X_{p}^{\gamma}
: for any
F\in X_{p}^{\gamma}
,

\displaystyle\begin{gathered}\sup_{\epsilon>0}\|F^{(\epsilon)}\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}+\sup_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\|\eta(j^{-1}\cdot)F\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\leq N(d,p,\gamma,\eta)\|h\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\,,\\
\lim_{\epsilon\rightarrow 0}F^{(\epsilon)}=\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\eta(j^{-1}\cdot)F=F\qquad\text{in}\quad X_{p}^{\gamma}\,.\end{gathered}

(A.32)

Step 1 :
\Lambda_{i}

Let
f\in X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)
.
It is implied by (
A.1
) and (
A.5
) that

\displaystyle\|f-\Lambda_{i}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}^{p}\lesssim\sum_{|n|\geq i-1}\big\|\big(\Psi^{-1}f\zeta_{0,(n)}\big)(e^{n}\cdot)\big\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}^{p}\leq\|f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}^{p}\,.

(A.33)

Therefore we have

\sup_{i}\|\Lambda_{i}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\leq N\|f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\quad\text{and}\quad\lim_{i\rightarrow\infty}\|f-\Lambda_{i}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}=0\,.

where
N=N(d,p,\theta,\gamma)
.

Step 2 :
\Lambda_{i,j}

Note that
\Psi^{-1}\Lambda_{i}f
and
\Psi^{-1}\Lambda_{i,j}f
are supported on

\big\{x\in\Omega\,:\,N(i)^{-1}\leq\rho(x)\leq N(i)\big\}\,,

It is implied by Proposition
A.3
.(8) and (
A.32
) that

\displaystyle\begin{gathered}\big\|\Lambda_{i}f-\Lambda_{i,j}f\big\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\simeq_{N_{2}}\big\|\big(1-\eta(j^{-1}\cdot)\big)\Psi^{-1}\Lambda_{i}f\big\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\rightarrow 0\quad\text{as}\quad j\rightarrow\infty\,,\\
\big\|\Lambda_{i,j}f\big\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\simeq_{N_{2}}\big\|\Psi^{-1}\Lambda_{i,j}f\big\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\lesssim_{N_{2}}\big\|\Psi^{-1}\Lambda_{i}f\big\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\simeq_{N_{2}}\big\|f\big\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\,,\end{gathered}

where
N_{2}=N(d,p,\gamma,\theta,i,\eta)
.

Step 3 :
\Lambda_{i,j,k}

Put

K_{i,j}=\{x\in\Omega\,:\,N(i)^{-1}\leq\rho(x)\leq N(i)\,,\,\,|x|\leq 2j\}\,,

and note that
K_{i,j}
is compact subset of
\Omega
.
Since
\Psi,\,\Psi^{-1}\in C^{\infty}(\Omega)
, Proposition
A.3
.(8) and (
A.1
) yield that if
g\in\mathcal{D}^{\prime}(\Omega)
is supported on
K_{i,j}
, then

\displaystyle\|g\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}:=\|\Psi^{-1}g\|_{X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\simeq_{N}\|\Psi^{-1}g\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\simeq_{N}\|g\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}},

(A.34)

where
N=N(d,p,\gamma,\theta,i,j,\Psi)
.
For any
k\in\mathbb{N}
,
\Lambda_{i,j,k}f
and
\Lambda_{i,j}
are supported on
K_{i,j}
.
Therefore it follows from (
A.32
) and (
A.34
) that

\displaystyle\|\Lambda_{i,j,k}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\,

\displaystyle\simeq_{N_{3}}\|\big(\Lambda_{i,j}f\big)^{(N(i)^{-1}k^{-1})}\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{N_{3}}\|\Lambda_{i,j}f\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\simeq_{N_{3}}\|\Lambda_{i,j}f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}\lesssim_{N_{3}}\|f\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}^{\gamma}(\Omega)}

and

\big\|\Lambda_{i,j}f-\Lambda_{i,j,k}f\big\|_{\Psi X_{p,\theta}}\simeq_{N_{3}}\big\|\Lambda_{i,j}f-\big(\Lambda_{i,j}f\big)^{(N(i)^{-1}k^{-1})}\big\|_{X_{p}^{\gamma}}\rightarrow 0\quad\text{as}\,\,k\rightarrow\infty\,,

where
N_{3}=N(d,p,\theta,\gamma,\Psi,i,j,\eta)
.
∎

A.3.
Equivalent norms

Proposition A.7
.

Let
\Phi
be a regular Harnack function,
p\in(1,\infty)
,
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
, and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}
.
There exists a constant
N=N(d,p,k,\theta,C_{2}(\Phi))
such that

\displaystyle\|\Phi f\|_{H_{p,\theta}^{k}(\Omega)}^{p}\simeq_{N}\sum_{m=0}^{k}\int_{\Omega}|\rho^{m}D^{m}f|^{p}\Phi^{p}\rho^{\theta-d}\,\mathrm{d}x\,.

Proof.

Make use of Proposition
A.3
.(7) and Lemma
3.12
.(3) to obtain

\displaystyle\|\Phi f\|_{\Psi H_{p,\theta}^{k}(\Omega)}\simeq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\|D^{i}(\Phi f)\|_{L_{p,\theta+ip}}\simeq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{L_{p,\theta+ip}}\,.

(A.35)

By (
A.35
), we only need to prove for
k=0
.
Since
\|f\|_{H_{p}^{0}}^{p}=\|f\|_{L_{p}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}^{p}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\Phi f\|_{L_{p,\theta}(\Omega)}^{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{n\theta}\int_{\Omega}|(\zeta_{0,(n)}\Phi f)(e^{n}x)|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f|^{p}\Psi^{p}\Big(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{n(\theta-d)}|\zeta_{0,(n)}|^{p}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\simeq_{d,\theta}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|f|^{p}\Phi^{p}\rho^{\theta-d}\,\mathrm{d}x

where the last inequality follows from (
A.2
).
∎

Proposition A.8
.

Let
\Phi
be a regular Harnack function,
p\in(1,\infty)
,
k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
,
\alpha\in(0,1)
, and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}
.
There exists a constant
N=N(d,p,k,\alpha,C_{2}(\Phi))
such that

\displaystyle\|\Phi f\|_{B_{p,\theta}^{k+\alpha}}^{p}\simeq_{N}\,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{\Omega}|\rho^{k}D^{k}f|^{p}\Phi^{p}\rho^{\theta-d}\,\mathrm{d}x

(A.36)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}\Big(\int_{|y-x|<\frac{\rho(x)}{2}}\frac{|D^{k}f(x)-D^{k}f(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}dy\Big)\Phi(x)^{p}\rho(x)^{(k+\alpha)p+\theta-d}\,\mathrm{d}x

Proof.

Step 1.
Our first claim is that

\displaystyle\|f\|_{B_{p,\theta}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}^{p}\simeq\,

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L_{p,\theta}(\Omega)}

(A.37)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}\int_{|x-y|\leq\frac{\rho(x)}{2}}\frac{|(\widetilde{\rho}^{(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}f)(x)-(\widetilde{\rho}^{(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}f)(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y\,.

We note the following equivalent norm of Besov spaces:

\displaystyle\|f\|_{B_{p}^{\alpha}}^{p}\simeq_{d,p,\alpha}\|f\|_{L_{p}}^{p}+\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|f(x)-f(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

(A.38)

(see,
e.g.
,
[
70
, Theorem 2.5.7/(i)]
).
Recall that for
\xi\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+})
, we denote
\xi_{(n)}(x)=\xi(e^{-n}\widetilde{\rho}(x))
.
From (
A.38
) we have

\displaystyle\|f\|_{B_{p,\theta}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}^{p}

\displaystyle\simeq_{N}

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{n\theta}\big\|\big(\zeta_{0,(n)}f\big)(e^{n}\,\cdot\,)\big\|_{p}^{p}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{n\theta}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{\big|\big(\zeta_{0,(n)}f\big)(e^{n}x)-\big(\zeta_{0,(n)}f\big)(e^{n}y)\big|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}\,.

Proposition
A.7
implies

I_{1}\simeq_{d,p,\theta}\|f\|_{L_{p,\theta}}^{p}\,.

Change of variables implies

\displaystyle I_{2}\,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|\zeta_{0,(n)}(x)f(x)-\zeta_{0,(n)}(y)f(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}e^{n(\theta-d+\alpha p)}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|\eta_{(n)}(x)F(x)-\eta_{(n)}(y)F(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\lesssim_{p}

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\iint_{\left\{|x-y|\geq\rho(x)/2\right\}}\frac{|\eta_{(n)}(x)F(x)|^{p}+|\eta_{(n)}(y)F(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\iint_{\left\{|x-y|\leq\rho(x)/2\right\}}\frac{|\eta_{(n)}(x)-\eta_{(n)}(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}|F(x)|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\iint_{\left\{|x-y|\leq\rho(x)/2\right\}}|\eta_{(n)}(y)|^{p}\frac{|F(x)-F(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle I_{2,1}+I_{2,2}+I_{2,3}\,,

where

F=\widetilde{\rho}^{\,(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}f\quad\text{and}\quad\eta(t)=t^{-(\theta-d)/p-\alpha}\zeta_{0}(t)\,.

Observe that for any
t>0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|\eta(e^{-n}t)|^{p}\simeq_{N}1\quad\text{and}\quad\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{-np}|\eta^{\prime}(e^{-n}t)|^{p}\lesssim_{N}t^{-p}\,,

(A.39)

where
N=N(d,p,\theta,\alpha)
.
It follows from (
A.39
) that

\displaystyle I_{2,1}\simeq_{N}\int_{\Omega}\int_{y:|x-y|\geq\frac{\rho(x)}{2}}\frac{|F(x)|^{p}+|F(y)|^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}x\simeq_{N}\int_{\Omega}|f(x)|^{p}\rho(x)^{\theta-d}\,\mathrm{d}x\,,

(A.40)

where
N=N(d,p,\theta,\alpha)
, and the last inequality is implied by that

|x-y|\geq\rho(x)/2\quad\Longrightarrow\quad|x-y|\geq\rho(y)/3\,.

To estimate
I_{2,2}
, observe that for
x,\,y\in\Omega
with
|x-y|<\rho(x)/2
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|\eta_{(n)}(x)-\eta_{(n)}(y)|^{p}\lesssim_{N}&\sum_{n}|x-y|^{p}e^{-np}\Big(\int_{0}^{1}|\eta^{\prime}(e^{-n}\widetilde{\rho}(x_{r}))|\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)^{p}\\
\leq\,\,\,\,&|x-y|^{p}\int_{0}^{1}\sum_{n}e^{-np}|\eta^{\prime}(e^{-n}\widetilde{\rho}(x_{r}))|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}r\\
\lesssim_{N}&|x-y|^{p}\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{\rho}(x_{r})^{-p}\,\mathrm{d}r\,,\end{split}

(A.41)

where
x_{r}=(1-r)x+ry
and
N=N(d,p,\theta,\alpha)
.
Here, the first inequality follows from that
|\nabla\widetilde{\rho}|
is bounded on
\Omega
, and the last inequality follows from (
A.39
).
Since
\rho(x_{r})\geq\rho(x)/2
, we have

\sum_{n}|\eta_{(n)}(x)-\eta_{(n)}(y)|^{p}\lesssim_{N}\,|x-y|^{p}\rho(x)^{-p}\,,

where
N=N(d,p,\theta,\alpha)
.
Consequently, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{2,2}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega}\int_{y:|x-y|\leq\frac{\rho(x)}{2}}\frac{|F(x)|^{p}\rho(x)^{-p}}{|x-y|^{d-(1-\alpha)p}}\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}x\lesssim_{d,\alpha,p}\int|f(x)|^{p}\rho(x)^{\theta-d}\,\mathrm{d}x.

(A.42)

Due to (
A.40
) - (
A.42
) and that

I_{2,3}\lesssim I_{2}+I_{2,2}\lesssim\|f\|_{B_{p,\theta}^{\alpha}}^{p}\,,

we have

\|f\|_{B_{p,\theta}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}^{p}\simeq\|f\|_{L_{p,\theta}(\Omega)}+I_{2,3}\,.

By applying (
A.39
) to
I_{2,3}
, (
A.37
) is proved.

Step 2.

Now, we prove (
A.36
) for
k=0
.
Denote
F:=\widetilde{\rho}^{(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}f
.
Since
\Phi\cdot\widetilde{\rho}^{(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}
is a regular Harnack function, if
|x-y|<\rho(x)/2
, then

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big|\big|\Phi(x)F(x)-\Phi(y)F(y)\big|-\Phi(x)\widetilde{\rho}(x)^{(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}\big|f(x)-f(y)\big|\Big|\\
\leq\,&\big|\Phi(x)\widetilde{\rho}(x)^{(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}-\Phi(y)\widetilde{\rho}(y)^{(\theta-d)/p+\alpha}\big||f(y)|\\
\leq\,&N|x-y|\cdot\Phi(y)\rho^{-1}(y)|F(y)|\end{split}

(A.43)

where
N=N(d,C_{2}(\Phi))
.
By combining (
A.37
) (for
\Psi F
instead of
f
), (
A.43
), and that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\int_{y:|x-y|<\rho(y)}\frac{\big(|x-y|\cdot\Phi(y)\rho^{-1}(y)|F(y)|\big)^{p}}{|x-y|^{d+\alpha p}}\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}x\lesssim\int_{\Omega}|f(y)|^{p}\Phi(y)^{p}\rho(y)^{\theta-d}\,\mathrm{d}y\,,

we obtain (
A.36
) for
k=0
.

Step 3
. Let
k\geq 1
.
The argument for (
A.35
) (see with Proposition
A.3
.(2)) also implies that

\|\Phi f\|_{B_{p,\theta}^{k+\alpha}(\Omega)}\simeq\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{B_{p,\theta+ip}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}+\|\Phi D^{k}f\|_{B_{p,\theta+kp}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}\,.

By Propositions
A.3
.(2) and (7), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{L_{p,\theta+ip}(\Omega)}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{B_{p,\theta+ip}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{H_{p,\theta+ip}^{1}(\Omega)}\simeq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{L_{p,\theta+ip}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{L_{p,\theta+ip}(\Omega)}+\|\Psi D^{k}f\|_{B_{p,\theta+kp}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}\,.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|\Phi f\|_{B_{p,\theta}^{k+\alpha}(\Omega)}\simeq\sum_{i=0}^{k}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{B_{p,\theta+ip}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}\simeq\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\|\Phi D^{i}f\|_{L_{p,\theta+ip}(\Omega)}+\|\Psi D^{k}f\|_{B_{p,\theta+kp}^{\alpha}(\Omega)}\,.

By Proposition
A.7
and the result of Step 2 ((
A.36
) for
k=0
), the proof is completed.
∎
